### [1] [Visual Text Compression as Measure Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06708)
*Lv Tang,Tianyi Zheng,Yang Liu,Bo Li,Xingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用测度传输理论分析视觉文本压缩中的信息损失，通过分解为精度代价和覆盖代价，实现无需下游标签的路由选择与聚焦机制，在24个NLP数据集上取得70.8%的oracle匹配率和+3.3%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文本压缩的token节省量无法预测下游任务表现，缺少一种原则性的、与任务相关的信息损失度量方法。

Method: 将文本和视觉token视为经验概率测度，提出ViT patch编码器诱导的推前映射，其传输代价分解为片内聚合的精度代价和跨片分割的覆盖代价，两者可通过无下游标签的探针估计。基于此设计路由准则和聚焦机制。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上评估24个NLP数据集，无标签路由准则在17/24（70.8%）数据集上匹配per-dataset oracle，相比纯LLM平均任务分数提升+3.3%，平均token减少-10.3%。

Conclusion: 测度传输理论为视觉文本压缩提供了原则性的信息损失度量框架，实现了无需下游标签的高效路由和自适应分辨率调整，有效弥补了压缩率与任务性能之间的预测鸿沟。

Abstract: Visual text compression (VTC) promises efficient long-context processing by rendering text into an image and re-encoding it with a vision-language model, often producing $3$--$20\times$ fewer decoder tokens than subword tokenization. Yet token savings do not translate predictably into downstream utility: on some tasks the visual path matches or exceeds the text path, on others it collapses, and the compression ratio itself does not predict which regime will occur. The missing quantity is therefore not another summary of efficiency, but a principled measure of task-relevant information loss induced by visual encoding. We address this problem by formulating VTC in the language of measure transport. Treating text and visual tokens as empirical probability measures, we show that the ViT patch encoder induces a push-forward map whose transport cost decomposes into a precision cost from within-patch aggregation and a coverage cost from cross-patch fragmentation. Both terms are estimable from downstream-label-free probes. This formulation yields two operational consequences: a downstream-label-free routing criterion that selects whether to use the visual path for a given input or benchmark instance, and a transport-informed foveation mechanism that re-encodes high-cost regions at higher resolution. Across $24$ NLP datasets at Qwen3-4B, our label-free rule matches the per-dataset oracle on $17/24$ datasets ($70.8\%$), and improves the average task score by $+3.3\%$ with $-10.3\%$ average tokens relative to a pure-LLM.

</details>


### [2] [HumanNet: Scaling Human-centric Video Learning to One Million Hours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06747)
*Yufan Deng,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HumanNet是一个百万小时规模的人类中心视频语料库，包含第一/第三人称视角、细粒度活动、人-物交互、工具使用和长时序行为，并提供了交互中心化的标注（字幕、运动描述、手部/身体信号）。实验表明，使用HumanNet中1000小时自我中心视频继续训练Qwen VLM的效果，优于使用100小时真实机器人数据（Magic Cobot），证明了人类视频可作为机器人数据的可扩展、低成本替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身智能领域，视觉和语言已通过互联网语料实现了规模化，但物理交互学习受限于缺乏大规模、多样化且富含标注的人类活动数据。现有机器人数据采集成本高、规模有限，亟需探索利用海量互联网人类视频来替代或补充机器人专用数据。

Method: 构建HumanNet百万小时级人类中心视频语料库，包含第一/第三人称视角、细粒度活动、人-物交互、工具使用和长时序行为，并提供交互中心化标注（字幕、运动描述、手部和身体信号）。引入系统化数据策展范式：人类中心过滤、时序结构化、视角多样性、标注增强。通过视觉-语言-动作消融实验验证：使用1000小时HumanNet自我中心视频继续训练Qwen VLM，与使用100小时Magic Cobot真实机器人数据对比。

Result: 在固定验证集上，使用HumanNet中1000小时自我中心视频继续训练的Qwen VLM模型，超过了使用100小时真实机器人数据（Magic Cobot）继续训练的效果，表明自我中心人类视频可作为机器人数据的高性价比替代方案。

Conclusion: 人类的视频可以成为机器人数据的可扩展、低成本替代品，为具身基础模型的规模化提供了新路径，即利用人类中心视频而非仅依赖机器人专用数据来训练具身基础模型。

Abstract: Progress in embodied intelligence increasingly depends on scalable data infrastructure. While vision and language have scaled with internet corpora, learning physical interaction remains constrained by the lack of large, diverse, and richly annotated human activity data. We present HumanNet, a one-million-hour human-centric video corpus that captures how humans interact with the physical world at scale. HumanNet spans both first-person and third-person perspectives and covers fine-grained activities, human-object interactions, tool use, and long-horizon behaviors across diverse real-world environments. Beyond raw video, the dataset provides interaction-centric annotations, including captions, motion descriptions, and hand and body-related signals, enabling motion-aware and interaction-aware learning. Beyond scale, HumanNet introduces a systematic data curation paradigm for embodied learning, where human-centric filtering, temporal structuring, viewpoint diversity, and annotation enrichment are treated as first-class design principles. This design transforms unstructured internet video into a scalable substrate for representation learning, activity understanding, motion generation, and human-to-robot transfer. We conduct a first-step validation on the value of this design through controlled vision-language-action ablation: under a fixed set of validation data, continued training from the Qwen VLM model with 1000 hours of egocentric video drawn from HumanNet surpasses the continued training with 100 hours of real-robot data from Magic Cobot, indicating that egocentric human video could be a scalable and cost-effective substitute for robot data. By building this project, we aim to explore the opportunity to scale embodied foundation models using human-centric videos, rather than relying solely on robot-specific data.

</details>


### [3] [R$^3$L: Reasoning 3D Layouts from Relative Spatial Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06758)
*Zhifeng Gu,Yuqi Wang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R³L框架，通过不变空间分解、一致空间想象和支持空间优化，提升3D布局生成中相对空间推理的可靠性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳相对空间推理中，反复的参考系变换会累积误差，导致语义和度量漂移，现有方法仅用后验启发式处理，结果不可靠。

Method: 提出三种策略：1）不变空间分解：解耦耦合的关系链；2）一致空间想象：通过想象-修正循环促进自一致性；3）支持空间优化：全局到局部的坐标重参数化简化位姿优化。

Result: 在多种场景类型和指令下，R³L生成更物理可行且语义一致的布局；分析表明解决帧引起的非一致性对可靠的多跳相对空间推理至关重要。

Conclusion: R³L框架有效提升了相对空间推理的可靠性，为解决3D布局生成中的参考系漂移问题提供了新思路，代码已开源。

Abstract: Relative spatial relations provide a compact representation of spatial structure and are fundamental to relative spatial reasoning in 3D layout generation. Recent works leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to infer such relations, but the inferred relations are often unreliable and are typically handled with post-hoc heuristics. In this paper, we propose R$^3$L, a general framework that improves the reliability and consistency of relative spatial reasoning for 3D layout generation. Our key motivation is that multi-hop reasoning requires repeated reference-frame transformations, which accumulate errors in inferred relations and lead to semantic and metric drift. To mitigate this, we propose invariant spatial decomposition to break coupled relation chains, and consistent spatial imagination to promote self-consistency through an imagine-and-revise loop. We further introduce supportive spatial optimization to ease pose optimization via global-to-local coordinate re-parameterization. Extensive experiments across diverse scene types and instructions demonstrate that R$^3$L produces more physically feasible and semantically consistent layouts. Notably, our analysis shows that resolving frame-induced inconsistencies is crucial for reliable multi-hop relative spatial reasoning. The code is available at https://github.com/Neal2020GitHub/R3L.

</details>


### [4] [LookWhen? Fast Video Recognition by Learning When, Where, and What to Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06809)
*Ali Salamatian,Anthony Fuller,Pritam Sarkar,James R. Green,Leonid Sigal,Evan Shelhamer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookWhen提出选择器-提取器框架，通过学习何时、何地、计算什么来降低视频处理的冗余，仅处理top-K tokens即可逼近全视频表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中充满冗余，但现有Transformer处理所有token的平方级计算成本过高，因此需要更高效的视频理解方法。

Method: 使用浅层选择器对缩放视频快速评分所有token，深层提取器只处理top-K tokens；通过最近邻距离排名进行选择预训练，并通过视频和图像教师蒸馏进行提取预训练。

Result: 在6个数据集上，LookWhen在9/12情况下实现了准确率-FLOPs的帕累托最优，速度比InternVideo2-B快6.7倍。

Conclusion: LookWhen通过选择性token计算，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低计算成本，验证了视频冗余可被有效利用。

Abstract: Transformers dominate video recognition. They split videos into tokens, and processing them has expensive superlinear computational cost. Yet videos are filled with redundancy, so we can question the need for this expense. We introduce LookWhen, a selector-extractor framework that factorizes video recognition into learning when, where, and what to compute. Our shallow selector gets a scaled-down video and quickly scores all tokens across space-time, while our deep extractor gets the top-K selected tokens to approximate full-video representations without actually processing all the tokens. A key challenge is defining effective supervision for selection and extraction. For selection pre-training, we introduce a score on representations that ranks tokens by uniqueness using a simple nearest-neighbor distance. For extraction pre-training, we distill both a video teacher and an image teacher, for which we normalize its frame-wise representations to learn what changes within videos. Through these strategies, our selector-extractor learns general and efficient representations for feature extraction or fine-tuning to a task. Through experiments on Kinetics-400, SSv2, Epic-Kitchens, Diving48, Jester, and Charades, we show that LookWhen achieves a better accuracy-computation trade-off than efficient models and upgraded baselines of similar size. LookWhen Pareto-dominates in accuracy-FLOPs on 9 of 12 cases (6 tasks x 2 settings) and roughly matches on 3. In accuracy-throughput, measuring time in practice, LookWhen is more efficient still at 6.7x faster than InternVideo2-B at equal accuracy.

</details>


### [5] [Knowledge Transfer Scaling Laws for 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06859)
*Ho Hin Lee,Dongna Du,Chu Wang,Yuankai Huo,Shi Gu,James C. Gee,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现3D医学影像跨模态预训练中，不同模态间知识迁移具有非对称性和可预测的幂律规律，据此提出基于缩放律的数据分配优化方法，识别出“枢纽-孤岛”结构，在多个下游任务上优于传统数据比例采样方法达58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学影像基础模型需要混合CT、MRI、PET等异质模态进行预训练，但当前的数据混合策略多基于启发式方法，缺乏理论指导，如何最优分配不同模态数据尚不明确。

Method: 1) 观察到不同医学影像模态在预训练中缩放速率不同，且跨模态知识迁移具有强非对称性；2) 发现MAE重建损失和跨域迁移均遵循可预测的幂律趋势；3) 将数据分配形式化为缩放律优化问题，求解得到"枢纽-孤岛"结构分配方案。

Result: 迁移感知的数据分配比数据比例采样提升高达58%，对未见预算的泛化相关性达r=0.989；在疾病分类、器官/病灶分割等下游任务中验证了更强的预训练表示。

Conclusion: 跨模态迁移的可预测性可以指导更优的数据分配策略，其中高迁移性模态应作为"枢纽"获得战略性分配，而孤立模态需要直接投入，这为3D医学影像基础模型训练提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are increasingly moving beyond 2D to volumetric domains such as 3D medical imaging, where unified pretraining across different imaging modalities (i.e. CT, MRI, and PET) could provide foundational models for diverse clinical tasks. However, training such models requires mixing heterogeneous imaging domains, and current mixture strategies remain largely heuristic. In this work, we observe that different medical imaging domains scale at variable rates during pretraining, and knowledge transfer between domains is strongly asymmetric: training on one domain can substantially improve another, but the reverse may be much weaker. Interestingly, both MAE reconstruction loss and cross-domain transfer follow predictable power-law trends with domain-specific behaviors. Motivated by these findings, we formulate data allocation as a scaling-law optimization problem. The derived allocations reveal an interpretable hub-and-island structure: highly transferable domains emerge as hubs that benefit many others and deserve strategic allocation, while isolated domains act as islands requiring direct investment. Empirically, transfer-aware allocation outperforms data-proportional sampling by up to 58% and generalizes well to unseen budgets with r=0.989. Downstream validation on disease classification and organ/lesion segmentation further confirms that the derived transfer-aware mixtures provide stronger pretrained representations for clinical 3D medical imaging tasks.

</details>


### [6] [AdpSplit: Error-Driven Adaptive Splitting for Faster Geometry Discovery in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06876)
*Yongjae Lee,Jingxing Li,Abhay Kumar Yadav,Rama Chellappa,Deliang Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdpSplit是一种基于误差驱动的自适应分裂算子，通过根据L1像素误差区域统计确定分裂子节点数量和初始化参数，替代3DGS中的固定分裂操作，在多个数据集上减少9.2%-22.3%训练时间，保持渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS中固定基数随机分裂需要多次致密化轮次才能发现精细细节，成为高效训练流程的瓶颈。

Method: 提出AdpSplit，一种误差驱动的自适应分裂算子，根据L1像素误差区域统计动态确定分裂子节点数量并初始化子节点参数。

Result: 在MipNeRF360、Deep-Blending和Tanks&Temples数据集上，作为即插即用替换，减少多个加速3DGS流程9.2%-22.3%训练时间；在FastGS上，MipNeRF360数据集PSNR匹配全调度训练，训练时间减少16.4%，相比原始3DGS加速12.6倍。

Conclusion: AdpSplit能有效减少训练时间并保持渲染质量，是一种简单有效的3DGS加速改进方案。

Abstract: Adaptive density control in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) repeatedly grows the Gaussian population through fixed-cardinality random splitting to discover useful scene structure. However, in vanilla 3DGS, its binary split operator requires many densification rounds to expose fine details, making it a bottleneck for efficient training schedules with fewer iterations. We introduce AdpSplit, an error-driven adaptive split operator that determines the number of split children and initializes the child parameters from L1-pixel-error region statistics, enabling fewer densification iterations, thus reduced training time, while preserving the rendering quality of full-schedule training. Across the MipNeRF360, Deep-Blending, and Tanks&Temples datasets, AdpSplit reduces the training time of multiple accelerated 3DGS pipelines by 9.2%-22.3% as a simple drop-in replacement for the standard split operator. With FastGS, AdpSplit matches the full-schedule PSNR on MipNeRF360 while reducing training time by 16.4%, corresponding to a 12.6x acceleration over vanilla 3DGS.

</details>


### [7] [TriDE: Triangle-Consistent Translation Directions for Global Camera Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06889)
*Francisco Chen,Yiran Wang,Yunpeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TriDE方法，通过相机-三角形一致性约束和消息传递机制，联合优化视角图中不一致的成对方向估计，显著提升方向精度和相机定位效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局SfM方法独立估计每对图像的方向，导致视角图中局部合理但全局不一致的问题。

Method: 利用相机-三角形一致性作为高阶验证信号，通过加权三角形上的消息传递机制，在不求解全局非线性优化的条件下优化成对方向。

Result: 在随机腐败模型下建立了精确恢复的相变界，在真实图像图上方向精度大幅提升，下游相机定位结果更优。

Conclusion: TriDE在局部成对估计与全局相机位姿几何之间建立了实用桥梁。

Abstract: Pairwise translation directions are a key input to camera location estimation in global structure-from-motion. Existing estimators usually process each image pair independently, producing directions that may be locally plausible but inconsistent with the other relative directions in the viewing graph. To jointly estimate the direction, we propose TriDE, which exploits camera-triangle consistency as an efficient higher-order verification signal. Instead of solving a costly global nonlinear optimization problem that is sensitive to initialization, TriDE refines unreliable pairwise directions through message passing between directions and their incident weighted triangles. This information propagation strategy enables us to establish a strong phase-transition bound for exact recovery under a realistic random corruption model. Experiments on real image graphs show that TriDE improves direction accuracy by a large margin and yields better downstream camera locations, providing a practical link between local pairwise estimation and global camera pose geometry.

</details>


### [8] [Towards Fairness under Label Bias in Image Segmentation: Impact, Measurement and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06891)
*Aditya Parikh,Stella Frank,Sneha Das,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向图像分割任务的数据中心型标签偏差检测与缓解框架，通过自信学习识别训练数据中的系统性标注错误，并利用特征空间中的子群分离性实现公平性能，无需干净标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分割中的标签偏差（特定子群上的系统性标注错误）尚未被充分探索，且现有检测方法依赖干净无偏的标注数据，而这类数据在实际中难以获取。

Method: 将自信学习（Confident Learning）适配到分割任务，通过比较训练标签与模型置信预测来隔离方向性误差；并利用编码器特征空间中子群分离性来缓解偏差。

Result: 在合成到真实偏差的三个数据集上，该方法无需干净标签即可可靠检测并缓解标签偏差，实现了各实验条件下的公平性能。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了分割任务中标签偏差的检测与缓解难题，摆脱了对干净无偏标注的依赖，为公平分割模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Labeled datasets reflect the biases of their annotation pipelines, which sometimes introduce label bias: group-conditional label errors that cause systematic performance disparities across demographic subgroups. Label bias in image segmentation remains underexplored, as even detecting it typically requires clean, unbiased annotations, which are not readily available. We present a data-centric adaptation of Confident Learning to segmentation, allowing detection of label bias directly in the training data without a clean, unbiased ground truth. By comparing the provided training labels to the model's confident predictions, we isolate directional errors that quantify the presence and nature of bias, where standard overlap metrics like Dice fail. We further show that label bias influences subgroup separability in the encoder's feature space, an artifact we leverage for bias mitigation rather than suppressing it. We evaluate three datasets, spanning from synthetic to real-life bias, showing how our framework reliably detects and mitigates bias without access to clean labels, achieving equitable performance across experimental conditions.

</details>


### [9] [Not All Tokens Need 40 Steps: Heterogeneous Step Allocation in Diffusion Transformers for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06892)
*Ernie Chu,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HSA（异构步长分配）算法，根据视频token的运动速度动态分配去噪步数，配合KV缓存同步机制和缓存欧拉更新，在不牺牲全局上下文的前提下大幅降低DiTs视频生成的计算成本，在50%和25%运行时下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer（DiTs）在视频生成中对所有时空token均匀分配去噪步数，计算成本极高，而人类视觉可以忽略大量冗余运动，作者受此启发希望实现非均匀的步长分配以降低计算开销。

Method: 1）基于速度动态为不同时空token分配异构的步数预算；2）引入KV缓存同步机制，使活跃token能关注完整序列而跳过非活跃token；3）推导缓存欧拉更新，单次操作即可推进跳过token的隐状态，无需额外模型评估。

Result: 在Wan-2和LTX-2模型上的T2V和I2V任务中，HSA在激进加速场景（50%和25%运行时）显著优于此前最先进的缓存方法和Flow Matching基线，实现了更优的生成质量-运行时帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: HSA是一种无需离线分析、无需额外训练的高效推理算法，能在严格计算预算下稳健保持视频的结构完整性和生成质量，为DiTs视频生成的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved state-of-the-art video generation quality, but they incur immense computational cost because standard inference applies the same number of denoising steps uniformly to every token in the sequence. It is well known that human vision ignores vast amounts of redundant motion. Why, then, do our densest models treat every spatiotemporal token with equal priority? In this paper, we introduce Heterogeneous Step Allocation (HSA), a training-free inference algorithm that assigns varying step budgets to different spatiotemporal tokens based on their velocity dynamics. To resolve the resulting sequence-length mismatch without sacrificing global context, HSA introduces a KV-cache synchronization mechanism that allows active tokens to attend to the full sequence while entirely bypassing inactive tokens. Furthermore, we derive a cached Euler update that advances the latent states of skipped tokens in a single operation without additional model evaluations. We evaluate HSA on the Wan-2 and LTX-2 models for both text-to-video (T2V) and image-to-video (I2V) generation. Our results demonstrate that HSA significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art caching methods and the vanilla Flow Matching baseline, especially at aggressive acceleration regimes (e.g., 50% and 25% runtimes). Crucially, HSA achieves a superior quality-runtime Pareto frontier without the need for expensive offline profiling, robustly preserving structural integrity and generation quality even under tight computational budgets.
  Project page: https://ernestchu.github.io/hsa

</details>


### [10] [Advancing Reliable Synthetic Video Detection: Insights from the SAFE Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06912)
*Kirill Trapeznikov,Gabriel Mancino-Ball,Jonathan Li,Paul Cummer,Jai Aslam,Danial Samadi Vahdati,Tai Nguyen,Matthew C. Stamm,Peter Bautista,Michael Davinroy,Laura Cassani,Jill Crisman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SAFE: Synthetic Video Detection Challenge的竞赛设计、数据集构建、评估方法及结果。竞赛旨在检测和识别由多种生成模型生成的合成视频，并评估其在常见后处理操作（如缩放、重压缩、运动模糊等）下的鲁棒性。竞赛共收到来自12个团队的600多份提交，使用了20小时、6000个视频样本。结果表明，跨生成器泛化能力有显著提升，但面对后处理伪影仍存在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频技术的快速发展使得合成媒体的检测与识别变得至关重要，亟需可靠的方法来区分真实视频与合成视频。

Method: 在Hugging Face平台上举办竞赛，设置两个任务：(1) 检测由多种最先进生成模型合成的视频；(2) 检测经过缩放、重压缩、运动模糊等常见后处理操作的合成视频。数据集包含13种现代高质量合成视频模型生成的视频，并与来自21个多样来源的真实视频匹配，总计20小时、6000个视频样本。

Result: 收到来自12个团队的600多份提交，展示了跨生成器泛化方面的显著进展，但模型对后处理伪影仍存在持续脆弱性。

Conclusion: 竞赛推动了合成视频检测技术的发展，验证了当前方法的进步与局限，为未来研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative video technologies has intensified the need for reliable methods to detect and characterize synthetic media. To address this challenge, we organized the \href{https://safe-video-2025.dsri.org}{SAFE: Synthetic Video Detection Challenge}, co-located with the \textit{Authenticity and Provenance in the Age of Generative AI (APAI) Workshop }at ICCV 2025. The competition invited participants to develop and evaluate algorithms capable of distinguishing real from synthetic videos under fully blind evaluation conditions with over 600 submissions from 12 teams over a 90 day span. Hosted on the Hugging Face platform, the challenge comprised two primary tasks: (1) detection of synthetic video content generated by diverse state-of-the-art models, and (2) detection of synthetic content following common post-processing operations such as resizing, re-compression, motion blur and others. The challenge data consisted of 13 modern high quality synthetic video models with generated content matched to real videos from 21 diverse and challenge sources, all adding up to 20 hours of 6,000 video samples. This paper describes the challenge design, dataset construction, evaluation methodology, and outcomes, offering insights into the generalization and robustness of contemporary synthetic video detection methods. Our findings highlight measurable progress in cross-generator generalization but also persistent vulnerabilities to post-processing artifacts. https://safe-video-2025.dsri.org

</details>


### [11] [A$^2$RD: Agentic Autoregressive Diffusion for Long Video Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06924)
*Do Xuan Long,Yale Song,Min-Yen Kan,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出A²RD，一种基于智能体自回归扩散架构的长视频合成方法，通过“检索-合成-精炼-更新”的闭环流程，分段合成并自我改进视频，在一致性和叙事连贯性上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频合成方法在长时间跨度下存在语义漂移和叙事崩塌问题，缺乏对一致性和连贯性的有效保障。

Method: A²RD采用智能体自回归扩散架构，包含三个核心组件：多模态视频记忆（跟踪视频进展）、自适应片段生成（切换生成模式）、层次化测试时自我改进（帧级和视频级双重优化），通过闭环流程分段合成并自改进。

Result: 在公开基准和新提出的LVBench-C基准上（1-10分钟视频），A²RD在一致性上提升高达30%，叙事连贯性提升20%，人工评估也表明运动和平滑度显著改善。

Conclusion: A²RD通过将创造性合成与一致性保持解耦，有效解决了长视频合成中的语义漂移和叙事崩塌问题，实现了更高质量的长视频生成。

Abstract: Synthesizing consistent and coherent long video remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods suffer from semantic drift and narrative collapse over long horizons. We present A$^2$RD, an Agentic Auto-Regressive Diffusion architecture that decouples creative synthesis from consistency enforcement. A$^2$RD formulates long video synthesis as a closed-loop process that synthesizes and self-improves video segment-by-segment through a Retrieve--Synthesize--Refine--Update cycle. It comprises three core components: (i) Multimodal Video Memory that tracks video progression across modalities; (ii) Adaptive Segment Generation that switches among generation modes for natural progression and visual consistency; and (iii) Hierarchical Test-Time Self-Improvement that self-improves each segment at frame and video levels to prevent error propagation. We further introduce LVBench-C, a challenging benchmark with non-linear entity and environment transitions to stress-test long-horizon consistency. Across public and LVBench-C benchmarks spanning one- to ten-minute videos, A$^2$RD outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 30% in consistency and 20% in narrative coherence. Human evaluations corroborate these gains while also highlighting notable improvements in motion and transition smoothness.

</details>


### [12] [XiYOLO: Energy-Aware Object Detection via Iterative Architecture Search and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06927)
*Tony Tran,Richie R. Suganda,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XiYOLO系列模型，通过能量感知的NAS搜索框架和复合缩放策略，在异构边缘设备上实现比YOLOv12更优的精度-能量权衡，PascalVOC上中型模型mAP50达86.15，GPU能耗降低20.6%，NPU能耗降低35.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能量感知NAS方法部署场景有限，且真实能耗因设备异构、测量成本高而难以优化，亟需一种自适应框架在满足异构边缘设备约束的同时保持检测精度。

Method: 提出能量自适应框架，包含：1) 能量感知的XiResOFA搜索空间；2) 两阶段能量估计器；3) 迭代搜索找到单一节能基架构；4) 通过复合缩放将基架构扩展为XiYOLO系列，支持稀疏硬件测量下的可解释精度-能量权衡。

Result: PascalVOC上XiYOLO中型模型mAP50达86.15，GPU能耗比YOLOv12m低20.6%，NPU低35.9%；COCO上小规模GPU能耗降低53.7%，NPU降低51.6%；两阶段估计器在2-20样本的少样本适应下比联合预测器样本效率更高。

Conclusion: XiYOLO系列在异构边缘设备上实现优异的能量-精度权衡，所提两阶段能量估计器显著提升少样本场景下的样本效率，验证了能量自适应NAS框架的有效性。

Abstract: Object detection on heterogeneous edge devices must satisfy strict energy, latency, and memory constraints while still providing reliable perception for downstream autonomy. Existing energy-aware NAS methods often target limited deployment settings, while real energy remains difficult to optimize because it is highly device-dependent and costly to measure. We address these challenges with an energy-adaptive framework that combines an energy-aware XiResOFA search space, a two-stage energy estimator, and iterative search to identify a single energy-efficient base architecture. We then apply compound scaling to transform this base design into the XiYOLO family across deployment budgets, enabling interpretable accuracy-energy tradeoffs under sparse hardware measurements. Experiments on PascalVOC, COCO, and real-device deployment show that XiYOLO achieves a stronger energy-accuracy tradeoff than YOLO baselines. On PascalVOC, the medium XiYOLO model reaches 86.15 mAP50 while reducing energy relative to YOLOv12m by 20.6% on GPU and 35.9% on NPU. On COCO, XiYOLO reduces energy relative to YOLOv12 by up to 53.7% on GPU and 51.6% on NPU at the small scale. The proposed two-stage estimator also improves sample efficiency over a joint predictor under few-shot adaptation with only 2-20 target-device samples.

</details>


### [13] [Bringing Multimodal Large Language Models to Infrared-Visible Image Fusion Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06969)
*Yuchen Guo,Junli Gong,Yao Lu,Xintong Xu,Yiuming Cheung,Weifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FuScore方法，利用多模态大语言模型生成连续质量评分而非离散等级，结合四维子维度一致性构建软标签，并通过三方目标函数优化，实现了与人类视觉偏好高度相关的红外-可见光图像融合质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IVIF评估方法存在过度优化无参考统计指标或全参考指标的问题，而基于人类评分的奖励建模仅使用标量回归，未能利用MLLM的推理能力或编码感知模糊性；直接引入MLLM的离散监督会导致相似质量的融合图像被划分到不同等级。

Method: 利用MLLM生成连续质量评分而非离散预测；利用四个IVIF特定子维度的一致性构建逐图像软标签，其锐度反映整体判断的共识度；提出三方目标函数：逐图像分布监督、同源对Thurstone保真度（方法级排序）、跨源对Thurstone保真度（场景级排序）。

Result: 大量实验表明，FuScore在人类视觉偏好相关性上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: FuScore通过连续评分、软标签构建和三方目标优化，有效解决了现有IVIF评估方法中离散监督导致的质量区分问题，实现了更精细的图像融合质量评估。

Abstract: Infrared-Visible image fusion (IVIF) aims to integrate thermal information and detailed spatial structures into a single fused image to enhance perception. However, existing evaluation approaches tend to over-optimize both hand-crafted no-reference statistics and full-reference metrics that treat the source images as pseudo ground truths. Recent IVIF reward-modelling efforts learn from human ratings but use scalar regression on aggregated scores, neither leveraging the reasoning of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) nor encoding per-image perceptual ambiguity in their supervision, but naively introducing MLLMs with discrete one-hot supervision likewise collapses fused images of similar quality into different rating levels. To address this, we introduce FuScore, which utilizes an MLLM to mimic human visual perception by producing continuous quality score, rather than discrete level predictions, enabling fine-grained discrimination among fused images of similar quality. We exploit the agreement among four IVIF-specific sub-dimensions to construct a per-image soft label whose sharpness reflects how consensual the overall judgment is. We further introduce a tripartite objective combining per-image distributional supervision, within-source-pair Thurstone fidelity for method-level ordering, and cross-source-pair Thurstone fidelity for scene-level ordering across scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FuScore achieves state-of-the-art correlation with human visual preferences.

</details>


### [14] [TRAJGANR: Trajectory-Centric Urban Multimodal Learning via Geospatially Aligned Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06990)
*Maria Despoina Siampou,Gengchen Mai,Ni Lao,Jinmeng Rao,Neha Arora,Cyrus Shahabi,Shushman Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrajGANR，一种面向人类移动轨迹的多模态自监督学习框架，通过连续神经表示实现轨迹与街景图像、地理位置的细粒度对齐，在多个城市理解任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间多模态自监督学习方法主要针对静态模态对（如卫星图像、街景图像和文本）设计，无法有效处理人类移动轨迹这种连续运动路径数据，而轨迹对城市理解至关重要但未被充分探索。

Method: 提出TrajGANR框架：1）学习轨迹的连续神经表示，可在路径任意点进行表征；2）基于该表示实现轨迹与街景图像的细粒度对齐（即使图像不在轨迹航点上）；3）联合对齐三种模态：轨迹、街景图像、地理位置。

Result: 在四个城市移动和道路理解任务上，TrajGANR持续优于现有地理空间MSSL框架和轨迹专用基础模型。消融实验表明多模态学习目标和细粒度对齐是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 细粒度地理空间对齐优于粗粒度聚合，地理空间多模态学习对城市理解任务至关重要，为轨迹数据与静态地理观测的融合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised learning (MSSL) has emerged as a key paradigm for pretraining geospatial foundation models. However, existing geospatial MSSL methods are mainly designed for static pairs of modalities, such as satellite imagery, street-view imagery, and text, where learning is driven by aligning observations from the same or nearby locations. This assumption breaks down for human mobility trajectories, which represent continuous movement along paths rather than discrete observations at individual locations. Although trajectories are important for urban understanding through their ability to capture human activity across roads, neighborhoods, and places over time, they remain largely underexplored in current geospatial MSSL frameworks. We present TrajGANR, a novel trajectory-centric geospatial MSSL framework that aligns continuous movement patterns with static, location-based observations. TrajGANR learns a continuous neural representation of trajectories at arbitrary points along each path, which enables fine-grained alignment with nearby street-view images, even when they are not co-located with any trajectory waypoints. We leverage this capability to introduce an MSSL objective that jointly aligns three modalities: trajectories, street-view images, and their geographic locations. We evaluate TrajGANR on four urban mobility and road understanding tasks. Across these tasks, TrajGANR consistently outperforms existing geospatial MSSL frameworks and a trajectory-specific foundation model. Ablation studies further demonstrate that our proposed MSSL objective and the multimodal learning framework are the primary drivers of these improvements, highlighting the importance of fine-grained geospatial alignment over coarser aggregation, as well as geospatial multimodal learning.

</details>


### [15] [LensVLM: Selective Context Expansion for Compressed Visual Representation of Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07019)
*Roy Xie,Dan Friedman,Donghan Yu,Bowen Pan,Christopher Fifty,Jang-Hyun Kim,Xianzhi Du,Zhe Gan,Vivek Rathod,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LensVLM是一种后训练推理框架，让VLM先扫描压缩图像，再通过学到的工具选择性展开相关图像，在4.3倍压缩下保持与全文本上界相当的精度，最高支持10.1倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM将文本渲染为图像处理可绕过分词，但压缩会导致字符缩小无法区分，精度急剧下降。

Method: 提出LensVLM框架，让模型先扫描压缩图像，再通过可学习的工具选择性地将相关图像展开为未压缩形式进行精细阅读。

Result: 在7个文本QA基准上，4.3倍压缩时精度与全文本上界相当，最高10.1倍压缩时优于检索式、文本和视觉压缩基线；在多模态文档和代码理解任务上也泛化良好。

Conclusion: 训练使视觉压缩对渲染选择更鲁棒，压缩越高模型越依赖展开内容而非不可靠的视觉阅读；文本展开适合渲染文本，高分辨率图像展开适合原生文档。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) offer the exciting possibility of processing text as rendered images, bypassing the need for tokenizing the text into long token sequences. Since VLM image encoders map fixed-size images to a fixed number of visual tokens, varying rendering resolution provides a fine-grained compression knob. However, accuracy deteriorates quickly as compression increases: characters shrink below the vision encoder's effective resolution, making them indistinguishable. To address this, we propose LensVLM, an inference framework and post-training recipe that enables VLMs to scan compressed images, then selectively expand only the relevant images to their uncompressed form via learned tools. Building on Qwen3.5-9B-Base, LensVLM maintains accuracy comparable to the full-text upper bound at 4.3x effective compression and outperforms retrieval-based, text- and visual-compression baselines up to 10.1x effective compression across seven text QA benchmarks. LensVLM also generalizes to multimodal document and code understanding tasks, with the accuracy gain over baselines growing as compression increases. Our analysis validates this approach: training makes visual compression robust to rendering choices, and as compression grows the model increasingly relies on expanded content rather than unreliable visual reading. The analysis also yields practical tool-choice guidance: text expansion is preferable for rendered text, while high-resolution image expansion suits native documents whose layout cues carry task-relevant information.

</details>


### [16] [OneViewAll: Semantic Prior Guided One-View 6D Pose Estimation for Novel Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07023)
*Yang Luo,Yan Gong,Yongsheng Gao,Jie Zhao,Xinyu Zhang,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OneViewAll框架，在仅有一张真实RGB-D参考图且无CAD模型的情况下，通过语义先验引导的Project-and-Compare范式实现高效6D物体姿态估计，在LINEMOD数据集上达到92.5% ADD-0.1精度，大幅超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际6D姿态估计场景中，往往只能获取每个物体单张真实RGB-D参考视图，且缺乏CAD模型。现有方法过度依赖显式3D模型或多视角数据，限制了可扩展性。本文旨在解决这一具有挑战性的单参考无模型姿态估计问题。

Method: 提出OneViewAll框架，采用Project-and-Compare范式替代CAD渲染：直接在投影等变空间中对齐参考与查询观测。渐进式整合三级语义先验：(1)类别与场景级先验用于高效假设初始化；(2)物体级对称性先验通过镜像融合完成几何补全；(3)补丁级先验用于判别式精化。

Result: 在LINEMOD数据集上，仅用单张真实参考视图即达到92.5% ADD-0.1精度，远超CVPR 2025基线One2Any的52.6%；在YCB-V、Real275、Toyota-Light上均取得一致提升，且推理延迟低。

Conclusion: 对称感知投影方法在处理对称、无纹理和遮挡物体方面具有显著效果，验证了语义先验引导的单参考姿态估计范式的有效性。

Abstract: In many practical 6D object pose estimation scenarios, we often have access to only a single real-world RGB-D reference view per object, typically without CAD models. Existing methods largely rely on explicit 3D models or multi-view data, which limits their scalability. To address this challenging single-reference model-free setting, we propose \textbf{OneViewAll}, a semantic-prior-guided framework that performs pose estimation via a novel Project-and-Compare paradigm. Instead of relying on computationally expensive CAD-based rendering, our method directly aligns reference and query observations within a projection-equivariant space. OneViewAll progressively integrates hierarchical semantic priors across three levels: (1) \textit{category- and scene-level} priors for efficient hypothesis initialization; (2) \textit{object-level symmetry} priors for geometry completion via mirror fusion; and (3) \textit{patch-level} priors for discriminative refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OneViewAll achieves \textbf{92.5\%} ADD-0.1 accuracy on the LINEMOD dataset using only one real reference view -- significantly outperforming the CVPR 2025 baseline One2Any (52.6\%). It also yields consistent improvements on YCB-V, Real275, and Toyota-Light while maintaining low inference latency. Our results underscore the efficacy of symmetry-aware projection in handling symmetric, texture-less, and occluded objects.

</details>


### [17] [Learning to Track Instance from Single Nature Language Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07064)
*Yaozong Zheng,Bineng Zhong,Qihua Liang,Shuimu Zeng,Haiying Xia,Shuxiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\tracker的自监督视觉-语言跟踪器，无需任何边界框标注，通过动态令牌聚合模块实现语言引导的目标跟踪，在多个基准测试中超越现有自监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言跟踪方法严重依赖边界框标注，标注成本高昂且难以扩展。本文旨在实现无需任何边界框监督的自监督VL跟踪，降低对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 提出动态令牌聚合模块，包含三步：1）基于锚点令牌从模板帧选择多个重要目标令牌；2）按注意力分数合并选中的目标令牌并聚合到语言令牌中，消除冗余视觉噪声；3）融合后的语言令牌作为引导信号从搜索帧提取潜在目标令牌并传播至后续帧，实现自监督学习。

Result: 在视觉-语言跟踪基准测试上，\tracker超越了当前最先进的自监督方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了无需边界框标注即可有效学习语言引导的跟踪表示，为自监督VL跟踪提供了新的建模思路。

Abstract: How to achieve vision-language (VL) tracking using natural language descriptions from a video sequence \textbf{without relying on any bounding-box ground truth}? In this work, we achieve this goal by tackling \textit{self-supervised VL tracking}, which aims to evaluate tracking capabilities guided by natural language descriptions. We introduce \textbf{\tracker}, a novel self-supervised VL tracker that is capable of tracking any referred object by a language description. Unlike traditional methods that equally fuse all language and visual tokens, we propose an efficient Dynamic Token Aggregation Module, which treats each visual token \textbf{unequally}. The module consists of three main steps: i) Based on an anchor token, it selects multiple important target tokens from the template frame. ii) The selected target tokens are merged according to their attention scores and aggregated into the language tokens, thereby eliminating redundant visual token noise and enhancing semantic alignment. iii) Finally, the fused language tokens serve as guiding signals to extract potential target tokens from the search frame and propagate them to subsequent frames, enhancing temporal prompts and encouraging the tracker to autonomously learn instance tracking from unlabeled videos. This new modeling approach enables the effective self-supervised learning of language-guided tracking representations without the need for large-scale bounding box annotations. Extensive experiments on VL tracking benchmarks show that {\tracker} surpasses SOTA self-supervised methods.

</details>


### [18] [Learning Visual Feature-Based World Models via Residual Latent Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07079)
*Xinyu Zhang,Zhengtong Xu,Yutian Tao,Yeping Wang,Yu She,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于残差潜在动作（RLA）的世界模型RLA-WM，通过流匹配预测RLA，在效率和性能上超越现有基于特征和视频扩散的世界模型，并应用于机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像生成的世界模型效率低、易产生幻觉，而基于视觉特征的世界模型虽更高效，但在复杂交互中因直接回归导致预测模糊或崩溃，且在高维特征空间进行生成建模仍具挑战。

Method: 从DINO残差中学习一种新的潜在动作表示——残差潜在动作（RLA），并基于流匹配预测RLA，构建RLA-WM。

Result: RLA-WM在仿真和真实世界数据集上优于最先进的基于特征和视频扩散的世界模型，且速度比视频扩散快数个数量级。基于RLA-WM开发的两种机器人学习技术（无动作视频学习、纯视觉RL框架）也取得良好效果。

Conclusion: RLA-WM是一种高效、可泛化的世界模型，为机器人学习提供新范式，尤其适合从离线视频中进行无监督策略学习。

Abstract: World models predict future transitions from observations and actions. Existing works predominantly focus on image generation only. Visual feature-based world models, on the other hand, predict future visual features instead of raw video pixels, offering a promising alternative that is more efficient and less prone to hallucination. However, current feature-based approaches rely on direct regression, which leads to blurry or collapsed predictions in complex interactions, while generative modeling in high-dimensional feature spaces still remains challenging. In this work, we discover that a new type of latent action representation, which we refer to as *Residual Latent Action* (RLA), can be easily learned from DINO residuals. We also show that RLA is predictive, generalizable, and encodes temporal progression. Building on RLA, we propose *RLA World Model* (RLA-WM), which predicts RLA values via flow matching. RLA-WM outperforms both state-of-the-art feature-based and video-diffusion world models on simulation and real-world datasets, while being orders of magnitude faster than video diffusion. Furthermore, we develop two robot learning techniques that use RLA-WM to improve policy learning. The first one is a minimalist world action model with RLA that learns from actionless demonstration videos. The second one is the first visual RL framework trained entirely inside a world model learned from offline videos only, using a video-aligned reward and no online interactions or handcrafted rewards. Project page: https://mlzxy.github.io/rla-wm

</details>


### [19] [InfoGeo: Information-Theoretic Object-Centric Learning for Cross-View Generalizable UAV Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07099)
*Hongyang Zhang,Maonnan Wang,Ziyao Wang,Hongrui Yin,Man OnPun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InfoGeo框架，利用信息瓶颈理论，通过最大化跨视角对象中心结构关系的一致性和最小化视角特定噪声，显著提升了跨视角地理定位在复杂场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法依赖全局特征对齐，在面对区域纹理变化、天气条件差异以及无人机视角下密集细粒度对象造成的视觉混乱时，存在严重的域偏移问题，鲁棒性和泛化能力不足。

Method: 受对象中心学习启发，提出InfoGeo框架，将优化重构为信息瓶颈过程，包含两个核心目标：(i) 通过对齐跨视角的对象中心结构关系来最大化视角不变信息；(ii) 通过跨视角知识约束来最小化视角特定噪声信号。

Result: 在多种基准数据集和挑战性场景下的广泛评估表明，InfoGeo显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: InfoGeo通过信息瓶颈视角有效解决了跨视角地理定位中域偏移和视觉杂乱问题，为GPS缺失环境下的精确定位与导航提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization (CVGL) is fundamental for precise localization and navigation in GPS-denied environments, aiming to match ground or UAV imagery with satellite views. While existing approaches rely on global feature alignment, they often suffer from substantial domain shifts induced by varying regional textures and weather conditions. This issue becomes even more pronounced in UAV-based scenarios, where the broader perspective inevitably introduces dense, fine-grained objects, creating significant visual clutter. To address this, we draw inspiration from Object-Centric Learning (OCL) and propose InfoGeo, an information-theoretic framework designed to enhance robustness and generalization. InfoGeo reformulates the optimization as an information bottleneck process with two core objectives: (i) maximizing view-invariant information by aligning the object-centric structural relations across views, and (ii) minimizing view-specific noisy signals through cross-view knowledge constraints. Extensive evaluations across diverse benchmarks and challenging scenarios demonstrate that InfoGeo significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [20] [Neurosymbolic Framework for Concept-Driven Logical Reasoning in Skeleton-Based Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07140)
*Talha Ilyas,Deval Mehta,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号主义框架REASON，将基于骨架的人体动作识别重构为基于运动基元的一阶逻辑推理，在保持竞争性识别性能的同时提供可解释的逻辑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于骨架的动作识别模型虽然表现优异，但大多是黑盒模型，难以解释其决策过程。

Method: 使用时空骨架编码器提取潜在运动表征，通过时空概念解码器映射为可解释的概念谓词（分离姿态中心和动态中心抽象），再通过可微一阶逻辑层组合成可读的逻辑规则，并与LLM描述的运动基元对齐。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D 60/120和NW-UCLA数据集上达到竞争性识别性能，同时提供基于逻辑结构的显式可解释性。

Conclusion: 神经符号推理是可解释的时空动作理解的有效范式。

Abstract: Skeleton-based human activity recognition has achieved strong empirical performance, yet most existing models remain black boxes and difficult to interpret. In this work, we introduce a neurosymbolic formulation of skeleton-based HAR that reframes action recognition as concept-driven first-order logical reasoning over motion primitives. Our framework bridges representation learning and symbolic inference by grounding first-order logic predicates in learnable spatial and temporal motion concepts. Specifically, we employ a standard spatio-temporal skeleton encoder to extract latent motion representations, which are then mapped to interpretable concept predicates via a spatio-temporal concept decoder that explicitly separates pose-centric and dynamics-centric abstractions. These concept predicates are composed through differentiable first-order logic layers, enabling the model to learn human-readable logical rules that govern action semantics. To impose semantic structure on the learned concepts, we align skeleton representations with LLM-derived descriptions of atomic motion primitives, establishing a shared conceptual space for perception and reasoning. Extensive experiments on NTU RGB+D 60/120 and NW-UCLA demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive recognition performance while providing explicit, interpretable explanations grounded in logical structure. Our results highlight neurosymbolic reasoning as an effective paradigm for interpretable spatio-temporal action understanding. Code: https://github.com/Mr-TalhaIlyas/REASON

</details>


### [21] [Qwen3-VL-Seg: Unlocking Open-World Referring Segmentation with Vision-Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07141)
*Yuan Yao,Qiushi Yang,Humen Zhong,Jiangning Wei,Yifang Men,Shuai Bai,Miaomiao Cui,Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen3-VL-Seg 是一种参数高效的开放世界指代分割框架，仅引入 17M 参数（约基座模型的 0.4%），通过将 MLLM 预测的边界框作为结构性先验，结合轻量级框引导掩码解码器，实现了强大的像素级指代分割性能，并构建了 SA1B-ORS 数据集和 ORS-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在开放世界视觉定位上表现强劲，但输出仅限于稀疏的边界框坐标，无法进行密集的像素级预测。已有的分割方法要么直接预测稀疏轮廓点导致边界不连续，要么依赖外部分割模型（如 SAM）带来架构和部署开销。

Method: 提出 Qwen3-VL-Seg，将 MLLM 预测的边界框作为语义结构先验，通过轻量级框引导掩码解码器进行像素级分割。解码器包含多尺度空间特征注入、空间-语义查询构建、框引导高分辨率像素融合和迭代掩码感知查询精炼四个模块。同时构建了 SA1B-CoRS 和 SA1B-DeRS 两个子集的数据集，以及 ORS-Bench 评估基准。

Result: 在指代表达式分割、视觉定位和 ORS-Bench 上，Qwen3-VL-Seg 在闭集和开放世界设置下均表现优异，尤其在语言密集型指令和分布外泛化上优势明显。在通用多模态基准上，分割适配后仍保持通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: Qwen3-VL-Seg 以极小的参数增量实现了强大的开放世界指代分割能力，并且不依赖外部分割模型，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Open-world referring segmentation requires grounding unconstrained language expressions to precise pixel-level regions. Existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit strong open-world visual grounding, but their outputs remain limited to sparse bounding-box coordinates and are insufficient for dense visual prediction. Recent MLLM-based segmentation methods either directly predict sparse contour coordinates, struggling to reconstruct continuous object boundaries, or rely on external segmentation foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM), introducing substantial architectural and deployment overhead. We present Qwen3-VL-Seg, a parameter-efficient framework that treats the MLLM-predicted box as a semantically grounded structural prior and decodes it into pixel-level referring segmentation. At its core, a lightweight box-guided mask decoder combines multi-scale spatial feature injection, spatial-semantic query construction, box-guided high-resolution pixel fusion, and iterative mask-aware query refinement, introducing only 17M parameters (about 0.4\% of the base model). For scalable open-world training, we construct SA1B-ORS, an SA-1B-derived dataset with two subsets: SA1B-CoRS (category-oriented samples) and SA1B-DeRS (descriptive, instance-specific samples). For evaluation, we curate ORS-Bench, a manually screened benchmark with in-distribution and out-of-distribution subsets covering diverse referring expression types. Extensive experiments on referring expression segmentation, visual grounding, and ORS-Bench show that Qwen3-VL-Seg performs strongly across closed-set and open-world settings, with clear advantages on language-intensive instructions and strong out-of-distribution generalization. Evaluations on general multimodal benchmarks further show that the model broadly preserves general-purpose multimodal competence after segmentation-oriented adaptation.

</details>


### [22] [AGA3DNet: Anatomy-Guided Gaussian Priors with Multi-view xLSTM for 3D Brain MRI Subtype Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07142)
*Peiyu Duan,Xueqi Guo,Sepehr Farhand,Mehmet Berk Sahin,Xinyuan Zheng,James S. Duncan,Gerardo Hermosillo Valadez,Yoshihisa Shinagawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGA3DNet是一个基于报告引导的3D脑MRI分类框架，通过从放射学报告中提取解剖短语作为软先验通道，结合轻量级3D CNN和多视图xLSTM，实现准确的脑MRI亚型分类，并在回顾性队列中取得了优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的三维脑MRI亚型分类需要同时利用局部解剖线索和长距离上下文推理，现有方法在有效融合解剖先验知识方面存在不足。

Method: 从放射学报告中提取简短解剖短语，映射到图谱定义区域，通过符号距离变换和高斯加权转换为平滑空间先验，作为软解剖先验通道与轻量级3D CNN及多视图xLSTM聚合相结合。

Result: 在回顾性临床脑MRI队列上进行异常亚型区分，AGA3DNet在性能指标上取得了更好的整体平衡，并通过先验通道支持临床可解释的定位。

Conclusion: AGA3DNet通过报告引导的解剖先验有效提升了3D脑MRI分类性能，但存在单队列评估和缺乏大规模公开配对数据集的局限性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D brain MRI subtype classification benefits from both localized anatomical cues and long-range contextual reasoning. We present AGA3DNet, a report-grounded framework that incorporates brief anatomical phrases extracted from radiology reports as a soft anatomical prior channel and fuses it with a lightweight 3D CNN and multi-view xLSTM aggregation. Specifically, extracted anatomical phrases are mapped to atlas-defined regions and converted into smooth spatial priors using a signed-distance transform followed by Gaussian weighting, providing interpretable, anatomy-grounded guidance without requiring dense voxel annotations. We evaluate AGA3DNet on a retrospective institutional brain MRI cohort for abnormal subtype discrimination and compare against reproducible 3D classification baselines. AGA3DNet achieves improved overall balance across performance metrics and supports clinically interpretable localization through the prior channel. We discuss limitations related to single-cohort evaluation and the lack of large-scale public brain MRI datasets paired with radiology reports under broadly usable terms.

</details>


### [23] [TriP: A Triangle Puzzle Approach to Robust Translation Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07143)
*Zhekai Fan,Wanze Li,Jinxin Wang,Yunpeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriP，一种基于三角形的鲁棒平移平均框架，通过从三角形几何推断局部边尺度并在对数域同步尺度，实现了高效、鲁棒的相机位置恢复，在合成和真实数据集上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移平均问题中，方向测量缺乏距离信息导致估计病态且对异常值敏感，现有方法难以同时处理对抗性、循环一致等结构化污染以及退化问题。

Method: 先利用三角形几何推断局部相对边尺度，再在对数域中同步重叠三角形的尺度，从而恢复全局一致的边长和相机位置。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，TriP在准确性和鲁棒性上大幅超越所有先前的平移平均方法。

Conclusion: TriP通过高阶三角形一致性实现了对结构化污染的抗性，无需额外反退化约束即可避免零解问题，且完全可并行、计算高效、可扩展到百万级相机图。

Abstract: Translation averaging aims to recover camera locations from pairwise relative translation directions and is a fundamental component of global Structure-from-Motion pipelines. The problem is challenging because direction measurements contain no distance information, making the estimation problem highly ill-conditioned and highly sensitive to corrupted observations. In this paper, we propose TriP, a triangle-based framework for robust translation averaging. TriP first infers local relative edge scales from triangle geometry, and then synchronizes the scales of overlapping triangles in the logarithmic domain to recover globally consistent edge lengths and camera locations. By leveraging higher-order consistency across triangles, the proposed method is robust to adversarial, cycle-consistent, and other structured corruptions. In addition, TriP avoids the collapse issue without requiring any extra anti-collapse constraints, since log-scale synchronization excludes the degenerate zero-scale solution by construction. These structural advantages enable a particularly strong theory for exact location recovery. On the practical side, TriP is fully parallelizable, computationally efficient, and naturally scalable to graphs with millions of cameras. Moreover, it outperforms all previous translation averaging methods by a large margin on both synthetic and real datasets.

</details>


### [24] [Uncovering and Shaping the Latent Representation of 3D Scene Topology in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07148)
*Haoming Wang,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现VLMs存在潜在3D拓扑地图但被非几何语义掩盖，通过交叉场景线性特征提取分离空间子空间，并基于Dirichlet能量引入正则化方法，在少量微调后显著提升空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索现代视觉-语言模型（VLMs）是否像人类一样形成3D空间的认知地图（拓扑保持的潜在表征），并解决非几何语义（如颜色、形状）对空间信息的掩盖问题。

Method: 1）通过交叉场景线性特征提取分离出干净的空间子空间；2）证明该潜在表征与场景3D高斯核图的拉普拉斯特征映射的对应关系；3）基于Dirichlet能量引入潜在正则化方法，在500步简单合成数据微调后应用于真实空间基准。

Result: 在涉及场景拓扑理解的空间任务中，该方法比标准SFT和竞争基线提升高达12.1%。

Conclusion: VLMs确实存在潜在拓扑地图，通过数学原理的正则化方法可以有效提取并利用这一空间表征，显著提升模型的3D空间推理能力。

Abstract: Decades of cognitive science establish that humans navigate environments by forming cognitive maps, defined as allocentric and topology-preserving representations of 3D space. While modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate emergent spatial reasoning from 2D egocentric inputs, it remains unclear whether they construct an analogous 3D internal representation. In this paper, we demonstrate that current VLMs do possess a latent topological map of 3D scenes, but it is heavily overshadowed by non-geometric visual semantics, such as color and shape. By isolating this spatial subspace through cross-scene linear feature extraction, we extract a clean spatial subspace that causally controls the model's spatial outputs. We mathematically shape this latent representation and prove its correspondence to the Laplacian eigenmaps of the scene's 3D Gaussian-kernel graph, converging to the physical 3D space in the continuous limit. Motivated by this geometric identification, we further introduce a mathematically principled latent regularization method for VLMs, based on Dirichlet energy. Applying this single-term regularizer to a minimal 500-step supervised VLM fine-tuning (SFT) on simple synthetic data yields significant improvements on real-world spatial benchmarks, outperforming standard SFT and competitive baselines by up to 12.1\% in spatial tasks involving scene topology understanding. Source code is available at https://github.com/pittisl/vlm-latent-shaping

</details>


### [25] [Real-IAD MVN: A Multi-View Normal Vector Dataset and Benchmark for High-Fidelity Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07149)
*Wenbing Zhu,Jianing Liang,Linjie Cheng,Yurui Pan,Zhuhao Chen,Qingwang Yan,Yudong Cheng,Jianghui Zhang,Mingmin Chi,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Real-IAD-MVN大规模多视角表面法线数据集，替代稀疏3D点云，结合2D图像与密集伪3D法线图进行微细几何缺陷检测，并引入基于重构的统一原型基线方法，结果表明该方法优于现有多模态融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测方法中，2D RGB图像对纹理和光照敏感但难以捕捉细微几何缺陷，3D点云虽能捕获宏观形状却因稀疏性无法检测微小瑕疵（如划痕、凹陷）。为解决这一根本性数据局限，作者引入高质量多视角表面法线数据集，替代稀疏3D数据。

Method: 升级采集系统，从五个不同视角捕获高保真表面法线图；提出基于重构的基线方法，从图像和法线图流中提取跨模态统一原型进行异常检测。

Result: 实验表明，密集多视角伪3D（表面法线）相比稀疏3D点云能显著提升检测性能；基于统一原型的基线方法超越了现有最先进的多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 密集多视角表面法线是替代稀疏3D点云的有效几何表示，能够使侧壁和遮挡缺陷变得可检测；新数据集和基线方法为推进几何异常检测提供了丰富潜力。

Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is critical for quality control, but existing methods struggle with subtle, geometric defects. Standard 2D (RGB) images are sensitive to texture and lighting but often miss fine geometric anomalies. While 3D point clouds capture macro-shape, they are typically too sparse to detect micro-defects like scratches or pits. We address this fundamental data limitation by introducing Real-IAD-MVN (Multi-View Normal), a large-scale industrial dataset. By upgrading our acquisition system, Real-IAD-MVN captures high-fidelity surface normal maps from five distinct viewpoints, replacing sparse 3D data entirely. This provides a comprehensive geometric representation at a micro-detail level, making previously invisible side-wall and occluded defects explicitly detectable. Our experiments, conducted on this new dataset, first provide evidence that incorporating dense, multi-view pseudo-3D (surface normals) yields significantly better detection performance than using sparse 3D point cloud data. To further validate the dataset and provide a strong benchmark, we introduce a baseline method based on reconstruction, which learns to extract cross-modal unified prototypes from the image and normal map streams. We demonstrate that this unified prototype approach surpasses existing state-of-the-art multimodal fusion methods, highlighting the rich potential of our new dataset for advancing geometric anomaly detection.

</details>


### [26] [PRIMED: Adaptive Modality Suppression for Referring Audio-Visual Segmentation via Biased Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07154)
*Yuchen He,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRIMED模型，通过受认知神经科学启发的偏向竞争理论，实现自适应模态抑制，在Ref-AVS任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多模态线索视为同质输入，易受无关或误导性模态影响，缺乏对不同模态相关性的动态建模能力。

Method: 提出PRIMED框架，包含三个关键组件：1）Modality Prior Decoder估计参照表达依赖的模态类型，生成自适应引导；2）Token Distiller提取紧凑全局视觉token，通过Competition-aware Cross-modal Fusion模块共享层级全局上下文；3）Spatial-Aware Semantic Alignment loss通过对比学习增强前景-背景判别。

Result: 在Ref-AVS基准上取得整体最优性能。

Conclusion: 受认知神经科学启发的模态竞争机制能有效提升多模态参照分割的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Referring Audio-Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS) seeks to localize and segment target objects in video frames based on visual, auditory, and textual referring cues. The task is challenging because the relevance of different modalities varies across referring expressions and scenes, while existing methods typically treat multimodal cues as homogeneous inputs for fusion, prompting, or reasoning, making them vulnerable to irrelevant or misleading modalities. To address this problem, we propose PRIMED, inspired by the biased competition theory in cognitive neuroscience, which explicitly models both visual perception and language-driven prior modulation, and enables more accurate Ref-AVS by adaptive modality suppression. Specifically, a Modality Prior Decoder first estimates whether the referring expression relies primarily on audio, vision, or their joint interaction, generating a modality prior to adaptively guide high-level attention. A Token Distiller further extracts compact global visual tokens from high-level features and shares them across Competition-aware Cross-modal Fusion modules to provide hierarchical global context. Additionally, we introduce a Spatial-Aware Semantic Alignment loss to further enhance foreground-background discrimination through contrastive learning. Extensive experiments on the Ref-AVS benchmark demonstrate that PRIMED achieves state-of-the-art overall performance.

</details>


### [27] [Masks Can Talk: Extracting Structured Text Information from Single-Modal Images for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07178)
*Kai Zheng,Hang-Cheng Dong,Jiatong Pan,Zhenkai Wu,Fupeng Wei,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S2M框架，直接从变化检测数据集的标签掩码中提取结构化文本特征（语义四元组：位置、类型、方式、数量），无需额外标注成本，通过两阶段训练实现视觉与文本的深度对齐，在加沙多类变化检测数据集上超越使用大语言模型的多模态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感变化检测方法存在两个问题：一是单模态深度学习方法常将语义变化与视觉相似但无关的变化混淆；二是多模态方法依赖文本辅助监督，但文本描述要么语义粗糙且非结构化，要么由模型生成而带有噪声。所有方法都忽视了一个简单事实：细粒度的变化语义早已隐含在变化检测数据集自带的真值标签掩码中。

Method: 提出S2M框架：(1) 将每个变化区域自动转录为语义四元组 (where, what, how, how many)，并转换为固定模板文本描述，提供精确、密集、无噪声的多模态监督；(2) 采用两阶段训练策略：先在遥感图像上微调获得领域鲁棒表示，再引入带双向对比损失的多模态解码器，实现视觉特征与结构化文本嵌入的深度对齐。

Result: 在新建的Gaza-Change-v2多类变化检测数据集上，S2M达到Sek=17.80%、F_scd=66.14%，显著超越包括使用大语言模型的多模态方法。

Conclusion: 验证了变化检测的标签掩码本身就能提供丰富、精确的变化语义信息（位置、类型、方式、数量），证明了"掩码可以说话"的理念。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection is pivotal for urban monitoring, disaster assessment, and environmental resource management. Yet, unimodal deep learning methods frequently confuse genuine semantic changes with visually similar but irrelevant variations. Recent multimodal approaches incorporate text as auxiliary supervision, but their descriptions are either semantically coarse and unstructured or model-generated and thus noisy. Critically, all of them overlook a simple fact: fine-grained change semantics are already implicitly encoded in the ground-truth mask labels that come standard with every change detection dataset. These masks know where the change happened, what the land-cover types were before and after, how the transition occurred, and how many objects were involved. In this paper, we propose S2M, a framework that obtains structured textual features directly from change labels at zero additional annotation cost. Specifically, each change region is automatically transcribed into a semantic quadruple (where, what, how, how many) and converted into several fixed-template text descriptions, providing precise, dense, and noise-free multimodal supervision. We adopts a two-stage training strategy to fine-tune on remote sensing imagery firstly for robust domain-specific representation, after which a multimodal decoder with a bi-directional contrastive loss is introduced to achieve deep alignment between visual features and structured textual embeddings. To validate our method, we construct Gaza-Change-v2, a new multi-class change detection (MCD) dataset about the Gaza Strip. On this MCD dataset, S2M achieves a Sek of 17.80\% and an F$_{\text{scd}}$ of 66.14\%, notably surpassing even multimodal methods that leverage large language models. Our work demonstrates that masks can indeed talk. They tell us exactly what, where, how, and how many changes have occurred.

</details>


### [28] [SatSurfGS: Generalizable 2D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Satellite Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07181)
*Min Chen,Wei Guo,Bin Wang,Wen Li,Tong Fang,Jinbo Zhang,Junqi Zhao,Hong Kuang,Han Hu,Xuming Ge,Qing Zhu,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SatSurfGS，一种基于2D高斯泼溅的可泛化稀疏视角卫星图像表面重建方法，通过三级局部几何可靠性建模和粗到细的属性预测框架，在卫星数据集上实现了优于现有方法的渲染质量、重建精度和跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角卫星图像表面重建中，多视图匹配的可靠性存在强空间异质性，受光照差异、弱纹理和重复纹理影响，几何约束稀疏且不可靠；同时缺乏可泛化的前馈式2DGS框架用于卫星表面重建。

Method: 构建粗到细的高斯属性预测框架，在特征学习、参数估计和训练优化三个层次显式建模局部几何可靠性。具体包括：置信度感知的单目多视图特征融合模块、跨阶段自一致性残差引导模块、以及置信度双向路由损失函数。

Result: 在卫星数据集上，与代表性可泛化基线和竞争性逐场景优化方法相比，SatSurfGS在渲染质量、表面重建精度、跨数据集泛化能力和推理效率方面均取得更优结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的三级可靠性建模和粗到细预测框架有效解决了稀疏视角卫星图像表面重建的挑战，证明了2DGS在前馈式可泛化卫星表面重建中的潜力。

Abstract: Sparse-view satellite image surface reconstruction remains highly challenging, fundamentally because the reliability of multi-view matching under satellite imaging conditions is strongly spatially heterogeneous. Affected by large photometric differences, weak textures, and repetitive textures, multi-view geometric constraints are often sparse, unevenly distributed, and locally unreliable. Although 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) is more suitable than 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for the explicit representation of continuous surfaces, research on generalizable feed-forward 2DGS frameworks for sparse-view satellite surface reconstruction is still lacking. To address this issue, we propose SatSurfGS, a generalizable sparse-view surface reconstruction method for satellite imagery based on 2DGS. The proposed method builds a coarse-to-fine Gaussian attribute prediction framework and explicitly models local geometric reliability at three levels: feature learning, Gaussian parameter estimation, and training optimization. Specifically, we propose a confidence-aware monocular multi-view feature fusion module to adaptively integrate monocular priors and multi-view matching features according to local confidence; a cross-stage self-consistency residual guidance module to stabilize stage-wise Gaussian parameter refinement using the residual between the rendered height map from the previous stage and the current-stage MVS height map, together with confidence information; and a confidence bidirectional routing loss to achieve differentiated allocation of geometric and appearance supervision. Experiments on satellite datasets show that the proposed method achieves improved rendering quality, surface reconstruction accuracy, cross-dataset generalization, and inference efficiency compared with representative generalizable baselines and competitive per-scene optimization methods.

</details>


### [29] [PicoEyes: Unified Gaze Estimation Framework for Mixed Reality with a Large-Scale Multi-View Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07188)
*Fuxin Duan,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PicoEyes是一个统一的眼神估计框架，能从单目或双目输入直接预测3D眼参数、眼部分割、光轴、视轴和深度图等所有关键属性，支持校准、预测和不同设备姿态，在大规模多视图近眼数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼神估计方法通常只预测部分参数，缺乏统一的端到端框架，且缺少大规模多视图近眼数据集，难以支持混合现实中鲁棒、泛化的眼神估计。

Method: 提出PicoEyes统一框架，端到端联合估计眼参数和深度图，同时处理校准、预测及不同设备姿态，支持单目或双目输入。

Result: 在无校准、有校准、重新佩戴后校准及预测等多种设置下，均显著优于现有学术和工业方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: PicoEyes建立了一种实用、端到端的眼神估计范式，为混合现实应用提供了鲁棒且泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: We present PicoEyes, a unified gaze estimation framework that directly predicts all key attributes of gaze, including 3D eye parameters, eye-region segmentation, optical axis, visual axis, and depth maps, from either monocular or binocular inputs. The framework simultaneously addresses calibration, gaze forecasting, and varying device postures, while also supporting 3D eye reconstruction via joint estimation of eye parameters and depth maps in an end-to-end manner. In addition, we introduce a large-scale multi-view near-eye dataset containing comprehensive 2D and 3D annotations under diverse conditions, including train, test, rewear-test, and calibration sessions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PicoEyes achieves state-ofthe-art performance, consistently outperforming both academic and industrial gaze tracking methods across nocalibration, calibration, rewear-after-calibration, and forecasting settings. This work establishes a practical, end-toend paradigm for robust and generalizable gaze estimation in mixed reality (MR) applications.

</details>


### [30] [Attention Transfer Is Not Universally Effective for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07191)
*Huaiyuan Qin,Muli Yang,Gabriel James Goenawan,Peng Hu,Chen Gong,Xi Peng,Hongyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 注意力迁移（Attention Transfer）在视觉Transformer（ViT）中并非普遍有效。在20个教师模型的综合基准测试中，11个ViT家族中7个成功迁移，4个持续失败，性能比从头训练的基线低5.1%。失败的原因是师生架构不匹配——即使学生能匹配教师的注意力模式，这些模式也无法正常工作。通过在标准学生中添加教师原生架构组件（随机初始化），可以完全逆转失败，但这些组件单独无法提升从头训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视先前研究发现的"仅迁移注意力模式即可从预训练教师ViT中获得完整收益"这一结论，验证其在不同ViT家族中的普适性。

Method: 在20个教师模型（来自11个知名ViT家族）上进行全面基准测试，通过注意力迁移训练标准学生ViT，分析成功与失败的情况。通过控制实验定位问题源，并通过添加教师原生架构组件验证机制。

Result: 注意力迁移在7个ViT家族中成功，但在4个家族中持续失败，性能下降高达5.1%。失败具有家族一致性，且不受训练时长、迁移数据集和分布外评估的影响。问题根源在于师生架构不匹配。

Conclusion: 注意力迁移的有效性依赖于学生与教师架构的匹配性。仅当学生架构与教师相同时，注意力模式才能被有效利用，这修正了先前对ViT中注意力表示的理解。

Abstract: A recent work shows that Attention Transfer, which transfers only the attention patterns from a pre-trained teacher Vision Transformer (ViT) to a randomly initialized standard student ViT, is sufficient to recover the full benefit of the teacher's pre-trained weights. We revisit this finding on a comprehensive benchmark of 20 teachers from 11 well-known ViT families and reveal that Attention Transfer is not universally effective. While 7 families transfer successfully, 4 consistently fail, falling up to 5.1\% below the from-scratch no-transfer baseline. Further results demonstrate that this failure is family-consistent across model sizes, and persists under extended training durations, different transfer datasets, and out-of-distribution evaluations. Controlled analyses then consistently localize the problem to the attention-routing channel, indicating that the key issue is not whether the student can match the teacher's attention patterns, but whether the matched patterns remain functional for the student. Crucially, we identify architectural mismatch between the pre-trained teacher and the standard student as the primary mechanism. By adding only the teacher's native architectural components to the student in a randomly initialized state, we completely reverse the failure for all 4 families. Notably, these components alone do not improve from-scratch training, confirming that they specifically unlock the usability of the teacher's attention. We further systematically show that this failure is not explained by the inadequate choice of transfer loss or by differences in pre-training recipes. Our findings refine the prevailing understanding of attention in ViT representations: attention is sufficient \textit{only} when the student architecture matches the teacher.

</details>


### [31] [AsyncEvGS: Asynchronous Event-Assisted Gaussian Splatting for Handheld Motion-Blurred Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07192)
*Jun Dai,Renbiao Jin,Bo Xu,Yutian Chen,Linning Xu,Mulin Yu,Tianfan Xue,Shi Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种灵活的高分辨率异步RGB-事件双相机系统及重建框架，通过事件数据重建清晰图像、跨域姿态估计和结构驱动事件损失，有效解决了严重运动模糊下的3D重建问题，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS和NeRF在输入图像存在严重运动模糊时性能下降，而传统事件辅助方法依赖低分辨率传感器和严格同步，难以用于智能手机等手持3D拍摄设备。

Method: 1) 从事件数据重建清晰图像；2) 基于VGGT的跨域姿态估计模块获得鲁棒初始化；3) 采用结构驱动事件损失和视图一致性正则化，缓解传统损失的病态行为。

Result: 在自建高分辨率异步RGB-事件数据集AsyncEv-Deblur及现有基准上均达到SOTA性能，显著提升了严重运动模糊下的重建鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提异步RGB-事件双相机系统及重建框架有效解决了运动模糊下的3D重建难题，拓展了事件辅助方法在手持设备上的实用性。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) achieve impressive photorealism but fail when input images suffer from severe motion blur. While event cameras provide high-temporal-resolution motion cues, existing event-assisted approaches rely on low-resolution sensors and strict synchronization, limiting their practicality for handheld 3D capture on common devices, such as smartphones. We introduce a flexible, high-resolution asynchronous RGB-Event dual-camera system and a corresponding reconstruction framework. Our approach first reconstructs sharp images from the event data and then employs a cross-domain pose estimation module based on the Visual Geometry Transformer (VGGT) to obtain robust initialization for 3DGS. During optimization, we employ a structure-driven event loss and view-specific consistency regularizers to mitigate the ill-posed behavior of traditional event losses and deblurring losses, ensuring both stable and high-fidelity reconstruction. We further contribute AsyncEv-Deblur, a new high-resolution RGB-Event dataset captured with our asynchronous system. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both our challenging dataset and existing benchmarks, substantially improving reconstruction robustness under severe motion blur. Project page: https://openimaginglab.github.io/AsyncEvGS/

</details>


### [32] [Closed-Form Linear-Probe Dataset Distillation for Pre-trained Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07194)
*Bincheng Peng,Guang Li,Ping Liu,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLP-DD方法，利用冻结特征的线性探针闭式解进行数据集蒸馏，避免轨迹匹配和NTK近似，在ImageNet上以更低的计算成本达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对冻结预训练编码器+线性探针设置的数据集蒸馏方法，要么需要展开迭代线性探针更新进行轨迹匹配，要么依赖为从头训练设计的NTK近似闭式解，二者均未利用冻结特征线性探针存在精确闭式解这一特性。

Method: 提出双层级优化框架CLP-DD：内层通过样本空间核岭回归求解器计算合成集诱导的线性探针闭式解，外层以温度缩放softmax交叉熵评估该分类器在真实特征上的表现，分类器列向量作为特征空间中的学习类锚点。

Result: ImageNet-100上四个预训练骨干显著优于LGM without DSA，接近LGM with DSA。ImageNet-1K上三个骨干匹配或超越LGM with DSA，速度约快14倍，GPU内存使用不到1/8。

Conclusion: 外层损失函数的选择至关重要：闭式内层求解器搭配标准MSE外损效果远不如轨迹方法，而判别性外损几乎完全缩小差距。该方法在保持性能的同时大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses a large training set into a small synthetic set that preserves downstream training utility. While most existing methods target training networks from scratch, modern visual transfer learning often uses frozen pre-trained encoders followed by lightweight linear probing. Existing distillation methods for this setting either unroll iterative linear-probe updates with trajectory-based gradient matching, or rely on closed-form formulations originally designed for from-scratch training with neural-tangent-kernel (NTK) approximations. Neither route exploits the fact that frozen-feature linear probing admits a closed-form solution determined directly by the pre-trained features themselves, with no infinite-width approximation and no inner-loop trajectory. We propose Closed-Form Linear-Probe Dataset Distillation (CLP-DD), a bilevel formulation that computes the linear probe induced by the synthetic set with a sample-space kernel ridge solver. The synthetic images are then updated by evaluating this induced classifier on real features through a temperature-scaled softmax cross-entropy, where the classifier columns act as learned class anchors in feature space. We further show that the choice of outer objective is decisive: pairing the closed-form inner solver with a standard MSE outer loss substantially underperforms trajectory-based methods, while the discriminative outer loss closes most of the gap. On ImageNet-100 with four pre-trained backbones, CLP-DD substantially improves over LGM without DSA and approaches LGM with DSA at a fraction of the computational cost. On ImageNet-1K, CLP-DD matches or surpasses LGM with DSA on three of four backbones while running roughly $14\times$ faster and using less than one-eighth of the GPU memory.

</details>


### [33] [See Tomorrow, Act Today: Foresight-Driven Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07195)
*Bozhou Zhang,Nan Song,Yuang Wang,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeSight是一个基于世界模型的自动驾驶规划框架，通过先想象未来场景再做出决策（而非像传统方法那样基于当前观测直接反应），实现了预测性规划，在NAVSIM和nuScenes数据集上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶规划器本质上是反应式的，仅依赖历史和当前观测来预测未来动作。作者认为自动驾驶体应像人类司机一样，先在大脑中模拟"接下来会发生什么"，再据此采取行动。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 通过预训练的世界模型生成合理的未来视觉场景；(2) 基于这些想象出来的未来场景条件化地规划动作。将问题从"我现在该做什么"转变为"将会发生什么，我该如何应对"。

Result: 在NAVSIM和nuScenes数据集上的大量实验表明，显式的未来想象显著优于先前的最先进方法，验证了基于预见性驱动的方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将决策建立在对未来情境的预期而非仅当前观测之上，ForeSight能够更有效地应对动态、交互性场景，实现了从反应式规划到预测性规划的范式转变。

Abstract: Current end-to-end autonomous driving planners are fundamentally reactive: they condition on historical and present observations to predict future actions. We argue that autonomous agents should instead imagine future scenes before deciding, just as human drivers mentally simulate ``what will happen next" before acting. We introduce ForeSight, a foundation world model centric planning framework that reframes autonomous driving as anticipatory decision-making. Rather than treating world models as auxiliary components, ForeSight makes future scene imagination the primary driver of action prediction. Our approach operates in two stages: (1) generating plausible future visual worlds via a pretrained world model, and (2) planning actions conditioned on these imagined futures. This paradigm shift from ``what should I do now?" to ``what will happen, and how should I respond?" enables genuinely anticipatory rather than reactive planning. By grounding decisions in anticipated contexts rather than present observations alone, ForeSight navigates dynamic, interactive scenarios more effectively. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM and nuScenes demonstrate that explicit future imagination significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art alternatives, validating our foresight-driven approach.

</details>


### [34] [From Pixels to Primitives: Scene Change Detection in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07203)
*Chamuditha Jayanga Galappaththige,Jason Lai,Timothy Patten,Donald Dansereau,Niko Suenderhauf,Dimity Miller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-DIFF方法，直接在3D高斯原语空间而非渲染后的像素空间进行场景变化检测，通过各向异性漂移模型和可观测性项解决原语不一致问题，在真实基准上mIoU提升约17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的场景变化检测方法都遵循"先渲染再比较"的像素范式，但高斯泼溅表示本身的非约束性导致独立优化得到的原语在位置、形状、颜色上存在差异，使得在原语空间直接比较变得困难。作者希望直接利用原语属性本身进行变化检测，避免像素级方法的多视图不一致问题。

Method: 提出GS-DIFF方法：1) 构建各向异性几何漂移模型和光度漂移模型，以应对独立优化导致的原语不一致性；2) 引入逐原语可观测性项，反映每个高斯体受相机几何约束的程度；3) 直接在原语空间进行比较，不经过渲染步骤。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上，GS-DIFF相比先前最先进方法在平均交并比(mIoU)上提升了约17%。

Conclusion: GS-DIFF具有两个独特优势：1) 变化图天然具有多视图一致性，无需额外优化目标学习；2) 能够区分几何变化（如新增物体）和表面变化（如颜色改变），无需监督或外部模型，实现了变化类型的自动识别。

Abstract: Scene change detection methods built on Gaussian splatting universally follow a render-then-compare paradigm: the pre-change scene is rendered into 2D and compared against post-change images via pixel or feature residuals. This change detection problem with Gaussian Splatting has been treated as a question about pixels; we treat it as a question about primitives. We provide direct evidence that native primitive attributes alone -- position, anisotropic covariance, and color -- carry sufficient signal for scene change detection. What makes primitive-space comparison hard is the under-constrained nature of Gaussian splatting representation: independent optimizations yield primitive solutions whose count, positions, shapes, and colors differ even where nothing has changed. We address this challenge with anisotropic models of geometric and photometric drift, complemented by a per-primitive observability term that reflects the extent to which each Gaussian is constrained by the camera geometry. Operating directly on primitives gives our method, GD-DIFF, two properties that distinguish it from render-then-compare methods. First, change maps are multi-view consistent by construction, where prior work had to learn this through an additional optimization objective. Second, geometric and appearance changes are scored separately, identifying not just where but what kind of change occurred, distinguishing structural changes (e.g., an added object) from surface-level ones (e.g., a color change) without supervision or external model dependencies. On real-world benchmarks, GS-DIFF surpasses the prior state-of-the-art approach by approximatelt 17% in mean Intersection over Union.

</details>


### [35] [CASCADE: Context-Aware Relaxation for Speculative Image Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07230)
*Selin Yildirim,Subhajit Dutta Chowdhury,Mohammad Mahdi Kamani,Vikram Appia,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CASCADE方法，通过利用树状推测解码中目标模型隐藏状态表示的冗余性（语义可互换性和收敛性），实现了最高3.6倍的文本到图像生成加速，同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归图像生成虽然效果优异但计算成本高、速度慢，现有推测解码方法在图像生成领域效率提升有限，主要原因是目标模型在图像生成过程中不确定性高，导致草稿令牌拒绝率高。

Method: 形式化定义了树状推测解码中目标模型行为的两个特性——语义可互换性和收敛性；利用这些冗余性实现接受松弛（无需额外训练）；将目标模型的冗余信号注入草稿模型训练中。

Result: 在多个文本到图像模型和草稿架构上，CASCADE实现了最高3.6倍加速，达到最先进的推测解码加速效果，同时保持图像质量和文本提示保真度。

Conclusion: 通过识别和利用目标模型隐藏状态的冗余模式，可以在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升自回归图像生成的推理效率，为推测解码在视觉领域的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation is a powerful approach for high-fidelity image synthesis, but it remains computationally demanding and slow even on the most advanced accelerators. While speculative decoding has been explored to mitigate this bottleneck, existing approaches fail to achieve efficiency gains comparable to those observed in text generation. A key limitation is the target model's high uncertainty during image generation, which leads to high draft token rejection rates. In this work, we identify previously overlooked patterns in the target model's behavior that emerge naturally in tree-based speculative decoding. Specifically, we formalize two properties, semantic interchangeability and convergence, arising from the redundancies in the target model's hidden state representations. By capturing these redundancies across the depth and breadth of the predicted token tree, our method identifies principled opportunities for acceptance relaxation without requiring additional training. Additionally, we enhance standalone drafter performance by injecting the redundancy signals from the target model into drafter training with minimal modification. We evaluate our approach across multiple text-to-image models and drafter architectures. Results show that CASCADE achieves state-of-the-art speedups for drafter-based speculative decoding, with up to 3.6x acceleration, while maintaining image quality and text-prompt fidelity.

</details>


### [36] [Towards multi-modal forgery representation learning for AI-generated video detection and localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07232)
*Dat Le,Khoa Nguyen,Xin Wang,Shu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态架构，融合LMM语义分支、时空视觉分支和多尺度局部伪造音频分支，实现AI生成视频的检测与细粒度时间定位，效果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测器存在两方面局限：（1）仅利用单模态或部分模态数据进行建模；（2）缺乏细粒度的时序伪造定位能力。这促使研究者开发更全面、更精细的检测工具。

Method: 提出核心架构，联合集成三个分支：LMM（大型多模态模型）语义分支、时空（ST）视觉分支、多尺度局部伪造（PS）音频分支，实现多模态联合检测与细粒度时序定位。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在检测性能上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该多模态方法能同时检测AI生成视频伪造并定位其细粒度时间边界，有效弥补了现有方法的不足。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have democratized video creation at scale. AI-generated videos, including partially manipulated clips across visual and audio channels, pose escalating risks of semantic distortion and misuse, which motivates the need for reliable detection tools. Most existing AI-generated video detectors remain limited by single- or partial-modality of data modeling and the lack of fine-grained temporal forgery localization. To address these challenges, our primary novelty introduces a core architecture that jointly integrates an LMM semantic branch with a spatio-temporal (ST) visual branch and a multi-scale partial-spoof (PS) audio branch. This multi-modal approach enables simultaneous detection and fine-grained temporal localization of partially manipulated AI-generated video forgeries.
